===== CMakeLists.txt =====
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.20)
project(ELE LANGUAGES CXX)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)
set(CMAKE_EXPORT_COMPILE_COMMANDS ON)

find_package(Vulkan REQUIRED)

add_subdirectory(external/glm)

option(USE_SYSTEM_GLFW "Use system GLFW instead of submodule" OFF)

set(TARGET_UNIT_TESTS OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
set(TARGET_HELLO_WORLD OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
set(TARGET_PERFORMANCE_TEST OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
set(TARGET_SAMPLES OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
set(TARGET_VIEWER OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
set(DOUBLE_PRECISION OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
set(GENERATE_DEBUG_SYMBOLS ON CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
set(OBJECT_LAYER_BITS 16 CACHE STRING "" FORCE)
set(USE_ASSERTS OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
set(CROSS_PLATFORM_DETERMINISTIC OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
set(ENABLE_ALL_WARNINGS OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
set(INTERPROCEDURAL_OPTIMIZATION OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
set(JPH_USE_DX12 OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
set(JPH_USE_VK OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
set(JPH_USE_MTL OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
set(JPH_USE_CPU_COMPUTE OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
set(BUILD_SHARED_LIBS OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)

add_subdirectory(external/jolt/Build)

if(USE_SYSTEM_GLFW)
    find_package(glfw3 REQUIRED)
else()
    set(GLFW_BUILD_DOCS OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
    set(GLFW_BUILD_TESTS OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
    set(GLFW_BUILD_EXAMPLES OFF CACHE BOOL "" FORCE)
    add_subdirectory(external/glfw)
endif()

add_subdirectory(Engine)
add_subdirectory(game)

target_include_directories(ELE PUBLIC
    ${Vulkan_INCLUDE_DIRS}
    ${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/include
    ${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/external/stb
    ${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/external/tinygltf
    ${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/external/Jolt
)

target_link_libraries(ELE PUBLIC
    Vulkan::Vulkan
    glfw
    glm::glm
    Jolt    
)

target_compile_definitions(ELE PUBLIC
    GLM_FORCE_RADIANS
    GLM_FORCE_DEPTH_ZERO_TO_ONE
    TINYGLTF_NO_STB_IMAGE_WRITE
)

find_program(GLSLC glslc REQUIRED)

file(GLOB_RECURSE GLSL_FILES
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/shaders/*.vert"
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/shaders/*.frag"
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/shaders/*.comp"
)

foreach(GLSL ${GLSL_FILES})
    get_filename_component(FILE_NAME ${GLSL} NAME)
    set(SPIRV "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/game/shaders/${FILE_NAME}.spv")
    add_custom_command(
        OUTPUT ${SPIRV}
        COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND} -E make_directory "${CMAKE_BINARY_DIR}/game/shaders"
        COMMAND ${GLSLC} -I "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/shaders/common" ${GLSL} -o ${SPIRV}
        DEPENDS ${GLSL}
        COMMENT "Shader compiling ${FILE_NAME}"
    )
    list(APPEND SPIRV_FILES ${SPIRV})
endforeach()

add_custom_target(Shaders ALL DEPENDS ${SPIRV_FILES})

===== Engine/include/Components/CameraComponent.h =====
#pragma once
#include "Scene/IComponent.h"
#include "Scene/GameObject.h"
#include "Core/Camera.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <glm/gtc/quaternion.hpp>

class CameraComponent : public IComponent
{
public:
    enum class Mode { FirstPerson, ThirdPerson };
    
    CameraComponent(Camera& targetCamera, Mode mode = Mode::FirstPerson)
        : m_camera(targetCamera), m_mode(mode)
    {}

    void onAttach(GameObject& owner) override { m_owner = &owner; }

    void setPitch(float degreesPitch) { m_pitch = degreesPitch; }

    void applyToCamera()
    {
        if (!m_active) return;

        LOG("applyToCamera: owner pos=" << m_owner->transform.position.x << "," 
            << m_owner->transform.position.y << "," << m_owner->transform.position.z);


        glm::vec3 basePos = m_owner->transform.position;
        float yawRad = glm::radians(m_owner->transform.rotation.y);
        float pitchRad = glm::radians(m_pitch);
        
        glm::vec3 forward;
        forward.x = cos(pitchRad) * sin(yawRad);
        forward.y = sin(pitchRad);
        forward.z = -cos(pitchRad) * cos(yawRad);
        forward = glm::normalize(forward);

        glm::vec3 camPos = (m_mode == Mode::FirstPerson)
            ? basePos + m_eyeOffset
            : basePos + m_eyeOffset - forward * m_armLength;

        m_camera.setPosition(camPos);
        m_camera.lookAt(camPos + forward);
    }

    Camera& getCamera() { return m_camera; }

    void setActive(bool active) { m_active = active; }
    bool isActive() const { return m_active; }

    void setEyeOffset(glm::vec3 offset) { m_eyeOffset = offset; }
    void setArmLength(float length) { m_armLength = length; }
    void setMode(Mode mode) { m_mode = mode; }

private:
    Camera& m_camera;
    GameObject* m_owner = nullptr;

    Mode m_mode;
    bool m_active = true;

    float m_pitch = 0.0f;

    glm::vec3 m_eyeOffset{0.0f, 1.6f, 0.0f};
    float m_armLength = 4.0f;
};

===== Engine/include/Components/CharacterComponent.h =====
#pragma once
#include "Scene/IComponent.h"
#include "Scene/GameObject.h"
#include "Physics/PhysicsSystem.h"
#include "Core/TimeSystem.h"
#include <Jolt/Physics/Character/CharacterVirtual.h>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>

class CharacterComponent : public IComponent
{
public:
    CharacterComponent(PhysicsSystem& physics, TimeSystem& time,
        float radius = 0.3f, float height = 1.8f, float mass = 80.0f)
        : m_physics(physics), m_time(time), m_radius(radius), m_height(height), m_mass(mass)
    {}

    void onAttach(GameObject& owner) override
    {
        m_owner = &owner;
        m_character = m_physics.createCharacterVirtual(owner.transform.position, m_radius, m_height, m_mass);
        m_previousPosition = m_currentPosition = owner.transform.position;
    }

    void syncTransform(float deltaTime)
    {
        float alpha = glm::clamp(m_time.getFixedAlpha(), 0.0f, 1.0f);
        m_owner->transform.position = glm::mix(m_previousPosition, m_currentPosition, alpha);
    }

    void fixedUpdate(float fixedDeltaTime) override
    {
        m_previousPosition = m_currentPosition;

        JPH::Vec3 velocity(m_moveInput.x, m_verticalVelocity, m_moveInput.z);
        m_character->SetLinearVelocity(velocity);
        m_physics.updateCharacter(m_character.GetPtr(), fixedDeltaTime);

        if (isOnGround())
            m_verticalVelocity = 0.0f;
        else
            m_verticalVelocity -= 9.81f * fixedDeltaTime;

        m_currentPosition = rawPosition();
    
        std::vector<JPH::BodyID> overlapping = m_physics.queryOverlappingBodies(rawPosition(), m_radius);

        std::vector<JPH::BodyID> currentTriggers;
        for (auto id : overlapping)
            if (m_physics.isSensor(id))
                currentTriggers.push_back(id);

        for (auto id : currentTriggers)
        {
            bool wasInside = std::find(m_previousTriggers.begin(), m_previousTriggers.end(), id) != m_previousTriggers.end();
            if (!wasInside)
                m_physics.getContactListener().notifyEnter(id, m_bodyIdPlaceholder);
        }
        for (auto id : m_previousTriggers)
        {
            bool stillInside = std::find(currentTriggers.begin(), currentTriggers.end(), id) != currentTriggers.end();
            if (!stillInside)
                m_physics.getContactListener().notifyExit(id, m_bodyIdPlaceholder);
        }

        m_previousTriggers = currentTriggers;
    }

    glm::vec3 getVelocity() const { return glm::vec3(m_moveInput.x, m_verticalVelocity, m_moveInput.z); }

    void setMoveInput(glm::vec3 worldSpaceHorizontal) { m_moveInput = worldSpaceHorizontal; }

    void jump(float jumpSpeed = 5.0f)
    {
        if (isOnGround())
            m_verticalVelocity = jumpSpeed;
    }

    bool isOnGround() const
    {
        return m_character->GetGroundState() == JPH::CharacterVirtual::EGroundState::OnGround;
    }

    glm::vec3 getPosition() const { return m_owner->transform.position; }

private:
    glm::vec3 rawPosition() const
    {
        JPH::RVec3 pos = m_character->GetPosition();
        return glm::vec3(pos.GetX(), pos.GetY(), pos.GetZ());
    }

    std::vector<JPH::BodyID> m_previousTriggers;
    JPH::BodyID m_bodyIdPlaceholder;

    PhysicsSystem& m_physics;
    TimeSystem& m_time;
    GameObject* m_owner = nullptr;
    JPH::Ref<JPH::CharacterVirtual> m_character;

    float m_radius, m_height, m_mass;
    glm::vec3 m_moveInput{0.0f};
    float m_verticalVelocity = 0.0f;

    glm::vec3 m_previousPosition{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 m_currentPosition{0.0f};
};

===== Engine/include/Components/InputComponent.h =====
#pragma once
#include "Scene/IComponent.h"
#include "Core/Input.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>

class InputComponent : public IComponent
{
public:
    explicit InputComponent(Input& input) : m_input(input) {}

    glm::vec2 getMoveAxis() const
    {
        glm::vec2 move(0.0f);
        if (m_input.isKeyPressed(Key::W)) move.y += 1.0f;
        if (m_input.isKeyPressed(Key::S)) move.y -= 1.0f;
        if (m_input.isKeyPressed(Key::D)) move.x += 1.0f;
        if (m_input.isKeyPressed(Key::A)) move.x -= 1.0f;

        move.x += m_input.gamepad().getAxis(GamepadAxis::LeftX);
        move.y += -m_input.gamepad().getAxis(GamepadAxis::LeftY);

        if (glm::length(move) > 1.0f)
            move = glm::normalize(move);

        return move;
    }

    glm::vec2 getLookDelta() const
    {
        float gamepadYaw = m_input.gamepad().getAxis(GamepadAxis::RightX) * m_gamepadLookSpeed * 0.05;
        float gamepadPitch = -m_input.gamepad().getAxis(GamepadAxis::RightY) * m_gamepadLookSpeed * 0.05;

        return glm::vec2(m_input.getMouseDeltaX() + gamepadYaw, m_input.getMouseDeltaY() + gamepadPitch);
    }

    bool isJumpJustPressed() const { return m_input.isActionJustPressed("Up"); }
    bool isFirePressed() const { return m_input.isActionPressed("Fire"); }
    bool isFireJustPressed() const { return m_input.isActionJustPressed("Fire"); }
    bool isInteractJustPressed() const { return m_input.isActionJustPressed("Interact"); }

    void setGamepadLookSpeed(float speed) { m_gamepadLookSpeed = speed; }

private:
    Input& m_input;
    float m_gamepadLookSpeed = 150.0f;
};

===== Engine/include/Components/RigidBodyComponent.h =====
#pragma once
#define GLM_ENABLE_EXPERIMENTAL

#include "Scene/IComponent.h"
#include "Scene/GameObject.h"
#include "Physics/PhysicsSystem.h"
#include <Jolt/Physics/Body/BodyID.h>
#include <glm/gtc/quaternion.hpp>
#include <glm/gtx/euler_angles.hpp>
#include "Scene/Mesh.h"

class RigidBodyComponent : public IComponent
{
public:
    enum class Shape { Box, Sphere, Mesh, ConvexHull };
    enum class Motion { Static, Dynamic };

    RigidBodyComponent(PhysicsSystem& physics, Shape shape, Motion motion,
        std::shared_ptr<Mesh> collisionMesh = nullptr,
        glm::vec3 halfExtentOrRadius = glm::vec3(0.5f), float mass = 1.0f)
        : m_physics(physics), m_shape(shape), m_motion(motion)
        , m_collisionMesh(collisionMesh), m_size(halfExtentOrRadius), m_mass(mass)
    {}

    void onAttach(GameObject& owner) override
    {
        m_owner = &owner;
        glm::vec3 startPos = owner.transform.position;
    
        switch (m_shape)
        {
            case Shape::Box:
                m_bodyId = (m_motion == Motion::Static)
                    ? m_physics.createStaticBox(startPos, m_size)
                    : m_physics.createDynamicBox(startPos, m_size, m_mass);
                break;

            case Shape::Sphere:
                m_bodyId = (m_motion == Motion::Static)
                    ? m_physics.createStaticSphere(startPos, m_size.x)
                    : m_physics.createDynamicSphere(startPos, m_size.x, m_mass);
                break;
            
            case Shape::Mesh:
            {
                std::vector<glm::vec3> positions;
                for (const auto& v : m_collisionMesh->getCpuVertices())
                    positions.push_back(v.pos);
            
                m_bodyId = m_physics.createStaticMesh(positions, m_collisionMesh->getCpuIndices(), startPos);
                break;
            }
        
            case Shape::ConvexHull:
            {
                std::vector<glm::vec3> positions;
                for (const auto& v : m_collisionMesh->getCpuVertices())
                    positions.push_back(v.pos);
            
                m_bodyId = m_physics.createDynamicConvexHull(positions, startPos, m_mass);
                break;
            }
        
            default: break;
        }

        m_physics.registerBody(m_bodyId, &owner);
    }

    void update(float deltaTime) override
    {
        if (m_motion == Motion::Dynamic)
        {
            m_owner->transform.position = m_physics.getBodyPosition(m_bodyId);

            glm::quat q = m_physics.getBodyRotation(m_bodyId);
            m_owner->transform.rotation = glm::degrees(glm::eulerAngles(q));
        }
    }

    JPH::BodyID getBodyId() const { return m_bodyId; }

private:
    PhysicsSystem& m_physics;
    GameObject* m_owner = nullptr;
    JPH::BodyID m_bodyId;

    Shape m_shape;
    Motion m_motion;
    std::shared_ptr<Mesh> m_collisionMesh;
    glm::vec3 m_size;
    float m_mass;
};

===== Engine/include/Components/TriggerComponent.h =====
#pragma once
#include "Scene/IComponent.h"
#include "Scene/GameObject.h"
#include "Physics/PhysicsSystem.h"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <functional>

class TriggerComponent : public IComponent
{
public:
    TriggerComponent(PhysicsSystem& physics, glm::vec3 halfExtent)
        : m_physics(physics), m_halfExtent(halfExtent)
    {}

    ~TriggerComponent()
    {
        m_physics.getContactListener().unregisterTrigger(m_bodyId);
    }

    void onAttach(GameObject& owner) override
    {
        m_owner = &owner;
        m_bodyId = m_physics.createTriggerBox(owner.transform.position, m_halfExtent);
        m_physics.registerBody(m_bodyId, &owner);
        m_physics.getContactListener().registerTrigger(m_bodyId, this);
    }

    void fireEnter(JPH::BodyID other) { if (onTriggerEnter) onTriggerEnter(other); }
    void fireExit(JPH::BodyID other) { if (onTriggerExit) onTriggerExit(other); }

    std::function<void(JPH::BodyID)> onTriggerEnter;
    std::function<void(JPH::BodyID)> onTriggerExit;

private:
    PhysicsSystem& m_physics;
    GameObject* m_owner = nullptr;
    JPH::BodyID m_bodyId;
    glm::vec3 m_halfExtent;
};

===== Engine/include/Core/Camera.h =====
#pragma once
#include <glm/glm.hpp>

class Camera
{
public:
    void initialize(glm::vec3 position);

    void processKeyboard(glm::vec3 direction, float deltaTime);
    void processMouse(float deltaX, float deltaY);

    void setAspect(float aspect) { m_aspect = aspect; }
    void setPosition(glm::vec3 pos) { m_position = pos; }

    void setRoll(float degreesRoll) { m_roll = degreesRoll; }
    float getRoll() const { return m_roll; }

    void lookAt(glm::vec3 target);

    glm::mat4 getViewMatrix() const;
    glm::vec3 getFront() const { return m_front; }
    glm::vec3 getRight() const { return m_right; }
    glm::vec3 getPosition() const { return m_position; }
    glm::vec3 getUp() const  { return m_up; }
    glm::mat4 getProjectionMatrix() const;

private:
    void updateVectors();

    glm::vec3 m_position{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 m_front{0.0f, 0.0f, -1.0f};
    glm::vec3 m_up{0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f};
    glm::vec3 m_right{1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f};
    glm::vec3 m_worldUp{0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f};

    float m_yaw = -90.0f;
    float m_pitch = 0.0f;

    float m_moveSpeed = 3.0f;
    float m_mouseSensitivity = 0.1f;

    float m_aspect = 16.0f / 9.0f;
    float m_fov = 45.0f;
    float m_nearPlane = 0.1f;
    float m_farPlane = 100.0f;
    float m_roll = 0.0f;
};

===== Engine/include/Core/Engine.h =====
#pragma once
#include "Vulkan/Renderer.h"
#include "Core/ResourceManager.h"
#include "Core/Input.h"
#include "Core/Camera.h"
#include "Scene/Scene.h"
#include "Physics/PhysicsSystem.h"
#include "Scene/ScreenSpaceDecal.h"
#include "Core/FogSettings.h"
#include "Core/ICameraController.h"
#include "Core/FreeFlyCameraController.h"
#include "Core/TimeSystem.h"
#include "Core/GltfModel.h"
#include <GLFW/glfw3.h>
#include <memory>
#include <string>


class Engine
{
public:
    void initialize(int width, int height, const std::string& title);
    void run();
    void shutdown();

    void addDirectionalLight(glm::vec3 direction, glm::vec3 color, float intensity);
    void addPointLight(glm::vec3 position, glm::vec3 color, float intensity);

    std::shared_ptr<Mesh> loadMesh(const std::string& path, bool keepCpuData = false);
    std::shared_ptr<Material> loadMaterial(const std::string& albedo, const std::string& normal,
        const std::string& metallic, const std::string& roughness);

    void setExposure(float exp) { m_renderer.setExposure(exp); }

    void setCameraController(std::unique_ptr<ICameraController> controller);

    void setFogEnabled(bool enabled);
    void setFogSettings(const FogSettings& settings);
    const FogSettings& getFogSettings() const;

    Scene& getScene()   { return m_scene; }
    Camera& getCamera() { return m_camera; }
    TimeSystem& getTime() { return m_time; }

    Input& getInput() { return m_input; }
    PhysicsSystem& getPhysics() { return m_physics; }

    void pause() { m_time.pause(); }
    void resume() { m_time.resume(); }
    void setTimeScale(float scale) { m_time.setTimeScale(scale); }

    GameObject& loadGltfObject(const std::string& path);

    void onFramebufferResize();

    void fixedUpdate(float fixedDt);

    void setAntialiasing(AAMethod method) { m_renderer.setAAMethod(method); }

    void setCameraControlMode(bool useController) { }

    void setGodRaySettings(const GodRaySettings& settings) { m_renderer.setGodRaySettings(settings); }

    std::shared_ptr<ScreenSpaceDecal> addScreenSpaceDecal(const std::string& texturePath,
        glm::vec3 position, glm::vec3 rotationEuler, glm::vec3 size,
        const std::string& opacityPath = "", const std::string& normalPath = "", const std::string& mrPath = "");

    std::shared_ptr<Material> createDefaultMaterial();        

private:
    void updateInput(float deltaTime);
    void createMaterialDescriptorSet(std::shared_ptr<Material> material);

    GLFWwindow* m_window = nullptr;
    bool m_cleaned = false;
    float m_lastFrameTime = 0.0f;

    TimeSystem m_time;
    Renderer        m_renderer;
    ResourceManager m_resources;
    Input           m_input;
    Camera          m_camera;
    Scene           m_scene;
    FogSettings m_fogSettings;
    std::unique_ptr<ICameraController> m_cameraController;
    LightData m_lights{};
    bool m_useCameraController = true;
    PhysicsSystem m_physics;
};

===== Engine/include/Core/FogSettings.h =====
#pragma once
#include <glm/glm.hpp>

struct FogSettings
{
    bool enabled = false;

    glm::vec3 scatteringColor{0.7f, 0.75f, 0.85f};
    float density = 0.04f;
    float heightFalloff = 0.15f;
    float heightStart = 0.0f;
    float maxDistance = 60.0f;
    int stepCount = 24;
    float ambientIntensity = 0.15f;
    float sunIntensity = 1.0f;
    float noiseScale = 0.15f;
    float noiseSpeed = 0.02f;
};

===== Engine/include/Core/GLFWGamepad.h =====
#pragma once
#include "Core/IGamepad.h"
#include <GLFW/glfw3.h>
#include <array>

class GLFWGamepad : public IGamepad
{
public:
    explicit GLFWGamepad(int jid) : m_jid(jid) {}

    void update() override;

    bool isConnected() const override;
    bool isButtonPressed(GamepadButton button) const override;
    bool isButtonJustPressed(GamepadButton button) const override;
    float getAxis(GamepadAxis axis) const override;

private:
    int toGLFWButton(GamepadButton button) const;
    int toGLFWAxis(GamepadAxis axis) const;

    int m_jid;
    GLFWgamepadstate m_currState{};
    GLFWgamepadstate m_prevState{};
    bool m_hasState = false;

    static constexpr float DEADZONE = 0.15f;
};

#pragma once

    int width = 0;
    int height = 0;
};

struct GltfDecal
{
    std::string texturePath;
    std::string opacityPath;
    std::string normalPath;
    std::string mrPath;
    glm::vec3 position{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 rotationEuler{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 size{1.0f};
    float metallic = 0.0f;
    float roughness = 0.6f;
};

struct GltfMaterial
{
    int albedoImage = -1;
    int normalImage = -1;
    int mrImage = -1;
    int aoImage = -1;
    float metallicFactor = 1.0f;
    float roughnessFactor = 1.0f;
    glm::vec4 baseColorFactor{1.0f};
    int lightmapImage = -1;
    bool hasLightmap = false;
};

struct GltfCollider
{
    std::string shape;
    std::string motion;
    float mass = 1.0f;
    glm::vec3 position{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 rotationEuler{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 size{1.0f};
};

struct GltfEntity
{
    std::string name;
    glm::vec3 position{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 rotationEuler{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 scale{1.0f};
};

class GltfModel
{
public:
    void load(const std::string& path);

    const std::vector<Vertex>& getVertices() const { return m_vertices; }
    const std::vector<uint32_t>& getIndices() const { return m_indices; }
    const std::vector<Submesh>& getSubmesh() const { return m_submesh; }
    const std::vector<GltfImage>& getImage() const { return m_images; }
    const std::vector<GltfMaterial>& getMaterials() const { return m_materials; } 
    const std::vector<GltfLight>& getLights() const { return m_lights; }
    const std::vector<GltfDecal>& getDecals() const { return m_decals; }
    const std::vector<GltfCollider>& getColliders() const { return m_colliders; }
    const std::vector<GltfEntity>& getEntities() const { return m_entities; }

private:
    void computeTangents();

    std::vector<Vertex> m_vertices;
    std::vector<uint32_t> m_indices;
    std::vector<Submesh> m_submesh;
    std::vector<GltfImage> m_images;
    std::vector<GltfMaterial> m_materials;
    std::vector<GltfLight> m_lights;
    std::vector<GltfDecal> m_decals;
    std::vector<GltfCollider> m_colliders;
    std::vector<GltfEntity> m_entities;
    bool m_hasTangents = false;
};

===== Engine/include/Core/Input.h =====
#pragma once
#include <GLFW/glfw3.h>
#include <unordered_map>
#include "Core/IGamepad.h"
#include <string>
#include <memory>
#include <vector>

enum class Key
{
    W, A, S, D, E, Q,
    Space, LeftShift, LeftControl,
    Escape, Tab, Enter,
    Num1, Num2, Num3, Num4, Num5,
    F1, F2, F3, F4, F5,
    Unknown
};

enum class MouseButton
{
    Left, Right, Middle,
    Unknown
};

struct InputBinding
{
    enum class Type { Keyboard, Mouse, Gamepad } type = Type::Keyboard;
    Key key = Key::Unknown;
    MouseButton mouseButton = MouseButton::Unknown;
    GamepadButton gamepadButton = GamepadButton::Unknown;

    static InputBinding fromKey(Key k) { return { Type::Keyboard, k, MouseButton::Unknown }; }
    static InputBinding fromMouse(MouseButton b) { return { Type::Mouse, Key::Unknown, b }; }
    static InputBinding fromGamepad(GamepadButton b) { return { Type::Gamepad, Key::Unknown, MouseButton::Unknown, b }; }
};

class Input
{
public:
    void initialize(GLFWwindow* window);
    void update();

    bool isKeyPressed(Key key) const;
    bool isKeyJustPressed(Key key) const;
    bool isKeyJustReleased(Key key) const;

    bool isMouseButtonPressed(MouseButton button) const;
    bool isMouseButtonJustPressed(MouseButton button) const;

    float getMouseDeltaX() const { return m_mouseDeltaX; }
    float getMouseDeltaY() const { return m_mouseDeltaY; }
    float getScrollDelta() const { return m_scrollDelta; }

    void setCursorLocked(bool locked);
    bool isCursorLocked() const { return m_cursorLocked; }

    void bindAction(const std::string& action, InputBinding binding);
    bool isActionPressed(const std::string& action) const;
    bool isActionJustPressed(const std::string& action) const;

    IGamepad& gamepad() { return *m_gamepad; }
    const IGamepad& gamepad() const { return *m_gamepad; }

private:
    int toGLFWKey(Key key) const;
    int toGLFWMouseButton(MouseButton button) const;
    bool rawKeyPressed(Key key) const;
    bool rawMousePressed(MouseButton button) const;

    static void scrollCallback(GLFWwindow* window, double xoffset, double yoffset);

    GLFWwindow* m_window = nullptr;

    double m_lastMouseX = 0.0;
    double m_lastMouseY = 0.0;
    float m_mouseDeltaX = 0.0f;
    float m_mouseDeltaY = 0.0f;
    bool m_firstMouse = true;
    bool m_cursorLocked = true;

    static inline float s_scrollAccumulator = 0.0f;
    float m_scrollDelta = 0.0f;

    std::unordered_map<int, bool> m_currKeyState;
    std::unordered_map<int, bool> m_prevKeyState;
    std::unordered_map<int, bool> m_currMouseState;
    std::unordered_map<int, bool> m_prevMouseState;

    std::unordered_map<std::string, std::vector<InputBinding>> m_actionBindings;

    std::unique_ptr<IGamepad> m_gamepad;
};

===== Engine/include/Core/PhysicsLayers.h =====
#pragma once
#include <Jolt/Jolt.h>
#include <Jolt/Physics/Collision/ObjectLayer.h>
#include <Jolt/Physics/Collision/BroadPhase/BroadPhaseLayer.h>

namespace Layers
{
    static constexpr JPH::ObjectLayer NON_MOVING = 0;
    static constexpr JPH::ObjectLayer MOVING = 1;
    static constexpr JPH::ObjectLayer NUM_LAYERS = 2;
}

namespace BroadPhaseLayers
{
    static constexpr JPH::BroadPhaseLayer NON_MOVING(0);
    static constexpr JPH::BroadPhaseLayer MOVING(1);
    static constexpr uint NUM_LAYERS = 2;
}

class ObjectLayerPairFilterImpl : public JPH::ObjectLayerPairFilter
{
public:
    bool ShouldCollide(JPH::ObjectLayer inObject1, JPH::ObjectLayer inObject2) const override
    {
        switch (inObject1)
        {
            case Layers::NON_MOVING:
                return inObject2 == Layers::MOVING;
            case Layers::MOVING:
                return true;
            default:
                return false;
        }
    }
};

class BPLayerInterfaceImpl : public JPH::BroadPhaseLayerInterface
{
public:
    BPLayerInterfaceImpl()
    {
        m_objectToBroadPhase[Layers::NON_MOVING] = BroadPhaseLayers::NON_MOVING;
        m_objectToBroadPhase[Layers::MOVING] = BroadPhaseLayers::MOVING;
    }

    JPH::uint GetNumBroadPhaseLayers() const override { return BroadPhaseLayers::NUM_LAYERS; }

    JPH::BroadPhaseLayer GetBroadPhaseLayer(JPH::ObjectLayer inLayer) const override
    {
        return m_objectToBroadPhase[inLayer];
    }

    #if defined(JPH_EXTERNAL_PROFILE) || defined(JPH_PROFILE_ENABLED)
        virtual const char* GetBroadPhaseLayerName(JPH::BroadPhaseLayer inLayer) const override
        {
            switch ((JPH::BroadPhaseLayer::Type)inLayer)
            {
                case (JPH::BroadPhaseLayer::Type)BroadPhaseLayers::NON_MOVING: return "NON_MOVING";
                case (JPH::BroadPhaseLayer::Type)BroadPhaseLayers::MOVING:     return "MOVING";
                default: return "INVALID";
            }
        }
    #endif

private:
    JPH::BroadPhaseLayer m_objectToBroadPhase[Layers::NUM_LAYERS];
};

class ObjectVsBroadPhaseLayerFilterImpl : public JPH::ObjectVsBroadPhaseLayerFilter
{
public:
    bool ShouldCollide(JPH::ObjectLayer inLayer1, JPH::BroadPhaseLayer inLayer2) const override
    {
        switch (inLayer1)
        {
            case Layers::NON_MOVING:
                return inLayer2 == BroadPhaseLayers::MOVING;
            case Layers::MOVING:
                return true;
            default:
                return false;
        }
    }
};

===== Engine/include/Core/TimeSystem.h =====
#pragma once

class TimeSystem
{
public:
    void update(float rawDeltaTime);

    float deltaTime() const { return m_paused ? 0.0f : m_rawDeltaTime * m_timeScale; }
    float unscaledDeltaTime() const { return m_rawDeltaTime; }

    float fixedDeltaTime() const { return m_fixedDeltaTime; }
    void setFixedDeltaTime(float dt) { m_fixedDeltaTime = dt; }

    void setTimeScale(float scale) { m_timeScale = scale; }
    float getTimeScale() const { return m_timeScale; }

    float getFixedAlpha() const { return m_accumulator / m_fixedDeltaTime; }

    void pause() { m_paused = true; }
    void resume() { m_paused = false; }
    bool isPaused() const { return m_paused; }

    double totalTime() const { return m_totalTime; }

    int consumeFixedSteps();

private:
    float m_rawDeltaTime = 0.0f;
    float m_timeScale = 1.0f;
    float m_fixedDeltaTime = 1.0f / 60.0f;
    float m_accumulator = 0.0f;
    double m_totalTime = 0.0;
    bool m_paused = false;

    static constexpr int MAX_FIXED_STEPS_PER_FRAME = 5;
};

===== Engine/include/Physics/PhysicsSystem.h =====
#pragma once
#include <Jolt/Jolt.h>
#include <Jolt/Core/JobSystemThreadPool.h>
#include <Jolt/Core/TempAllocator.h>
#include <Jolt/Physics/PhysicsSystem.h>
#include <Jolt/Physics/Character/CharacterVirtual.h>
#include "Core/PhysicsLayers.h"
#include <glm/gtc/quaternion.hpp> 
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <memory>
#include <Jolt/Physics/Collision/ContactListener.h>
#include <Jolt/Physics/Collision/Shape/SphereShape.h>
#include <Jolt/Physics/Collision/CollideShape.h>
#include <vector>
#include <unordered_map>
#include <functional>

struct RaycastHit
{
    bool hit = false;
    JPH::BodyID bodyId;
    glm::vec3 point{0.0f};
    float distance = 0.0f;
};

class TriggerComponent;
class GameObject;

class TriggerContactListener : public JPH::ContactListener
{
public:
    void registerTrigger(JPH::BodyID id, TriggerComponent* trigger) { m_triggers[id] = trigger; }
    void unregisterTrigger(JPH::BodyID id) { m_triggers.erase(id); }

    void OnContactAdded(const JPH::Body& body1, const JPH::Body& body2,
        const JPH::ContactManifold&, JPH::ContactSettings&) override
    {
        dispatch(body1.GetID(), body2.GetID(), true);
    }

    void notifyEnter(JPH::BodyID trigger, JPH::BodyID other) { dispatch(trigger, other, true); }
    void notifyExit(JPH::BodyID trigger, JPH::BodyID other) { dispatch(trigger, other, false); }

    void OnContactRemoved(const JPH::SubShapeIDPair& subShapePair) override
    {
        dispatch(subShapePair.GetBody1ID(), subShapePair.GetBody2ID(), false);
    }

private:
    void dispatch(JPH::BodyID a, JPH::BodyID b, bool entered);

    std::unordered_map<JPH::BodyID, TriggerComponent*> m_triggers;
};

class PhysicsSystem
{
public:
    void initialize();
    void update(float fixedDeltaTime);
    void cleanup();

    JPH::BodyInterface& bodyInterface() { return m_physicsSystem->GetBodyInterface(); }

    JPH::BodyID createStaticBox(glm::vec3 position, glm::vec3 halfExtent);
    JPH::BodyID createDynamicBox(glm::vec3 position, glm::vec3 halfExtent, float mass);

    JPH::BodyID createStaticSphere(glm::vec3 position, float radius);
    JPH::BodyID createDynamicSphere(glm::vec3 position, float radius, float mass);

    JPH::BodyID createStaticMesh(const std::vector<glm::vec3>& positions, const std::vector<uint32_t>& indices, glm::vec3 worldPosition);
    JPH::BodyID createDynamicConvexHull(const std::vector<glm::vec3>& positions, glm::vec3 worldPosition, float mass);

    JPH::BodyID createTriggerBox(glm::vec3 position, glm::vec3 halfExtent);

    JPH::Ref<JPH::CharacterVirtual> createCharacterVirtual(glm::vec3 position, float radius, float height, float mass = 80.0f);
    void updateCharacter(JPH::CharacterVirtual* character, float deltaTime);

    glm::vec3 getBodyPosition(JPH::BodyID id) const;
    glm::quat getBodyRotation(JPH::BodyID id) const;
    TriggerContactListener& getContactListener() { return m_contactListener; }

    void registerBody(JPH::BodyID id, GameObject* owner) { m_bodyOwners[id.GetIndexAndSequenceNumber()] = owner; }
    GameObject* getOwner(JPH::BodyID id) const
    {
        auto it = m_bodyOwners.find(id.GetIndexAndSequenceNumber());
        return (it != m_bodyOwners.end()) ? it->second : nullptr;
    }

    RaycastHit raycast(glm::vec3 origin, glm::vec3 direction, float maxDistance);

    std::vector<JPH::BodyID> queryOverlappingBodies(glm::vec3 position, float radius);
    bool isSensor(JPH::BodyID id) const;

private:
    std::unique_ptr<JPH::TempAllocatorImpl> m_tempAllocator;
    std::unique_ptr<JPH::JobSystemThreadPool> m_jobSystem;
    std::unique_ptr<JPH::PhysicsSystem> m_physicsSystem;

    BPLayerInterfaceImpl m_broadPhaseLayerInterface;
    ObjectVsBroadPhaseLayerFilterImpl m_objectVsBroadPhaseLayerFilter;
    ObjectLayerPairFilterImpl m_objectLayerPairFilter;

    TriggerContactListener m_contactListener;

    std::unordered_map<JPH::uint32, GameObject*> m_bodyOwners;

    static constexpr JPH::uint MAX_BODIES = 1024;
    static constexpr JPH::uint NUM_BODY_MUTEXES = 0;
    static constexpr JPH::uint MAX_BODY_PAIRS = 1024;
    static constexpr JPH::uint MAX_CONTACT_CONSTRAINTS = 1024;
};

===== Engine/include/Scene/GameObject.h =====
#pragma once
#include "Scene/Transform.h"
#include "Scene/Mesh.h"
#include "Scene/Material.h"
#include "Scene/IComponent.h"
#include <memory>
#include <vector>
#include <typeindex>

class GameObject
{
public:
    std::string name;
    Transform transform;
    std::shared_ptr<Mesh> mesh;
    std::vector<std::shared_ptr<Material>> materials;
    bool isDecal = false;
    bool isDynamic = false;

    void setMaterial(std::shared_ptr<Material> mat) { materials = { mat }; }
    std::shared_ptr<Material> getMaterial(int index) const
    {
        if (materials.empty()) return nullptr;
        if (index < 0 || index >= (int)materials.size()) return materials[0];
        return materials[index];
    }

    template<typename T, typename... Args>
    T& addComponent(Args&&... args)
    {
        auto comp = std::make_unique<T>(std::forward<Args>(args)...);
        T& ref = *comp;
        comp->onAttach(*this);
        m_components[std::type_index(typeid(T))] = std::move(comp);
        return ref;
    }

    template<typename T>
    T* getComponent()
    {
        auto it = m_components.find(std::type_index(typeid(T)));
        if (it == m_components.end()) return nullptr;
        return static_cast<T*>(it->second.get());
    }

    template<typename T>
    bool hasComponent() const
    {
        return m_components.find(std::type_index(typeid(T))) != m_components.end();
    }

    template<typename T>
    void removeComponent()
    {
        m_components.erase(std::type_index(typeid(T)));
    }

    void updateComponents(float deltaTime)
    {
        for (auto& [type, comp] : m_components)
            comp->update(deltaTime);
    }

    void fixedUpdateComponents(float fixedDeltaTime)
    {
        for (auto& [type, comp] : m_components)
            comp->fixedUpdate(fixedDeltaTime);
    }

private:
    std::unordered_map<std::type_index, std::unique_ptr<IComponent>> m_components;

};

===== Engine/include/Scene/Material.h =====
#pragma once
#include "Vulkan/Texture.h"
#include <string>

class Material
{
public:
    void load(vk::Device device, vk::PhysicalDevice physicalDevice,
        vk::CommandPool pool, vk::Queue queue,
        const std::string& albedoPath,
        const std::string& normalPath,
        const std::string& metallicPath,
        const std::string& roughnessPath);

    void loadFromPixels(vk::Device device, vk::PhysicalDevice physicalDevice, vk::CommandPool commandPool, vk::Queue queue,
        const unsigned char* albedoPx, int aw, int ah,
        const unsigned char* normalPx, int nw, int nh,
        const unsigned char* mrPx, int mw, int mh,
        const unsigned char* aoPx, int ow, int oh,
        const unsigned char* lightmapPx, int lw, int lh, bool hasLightmap);

    vk::ImageView getAlbedoView() const { return m_albedo.getImageView(); }
    vk::Sampler getAlbedoSampler() const { return m_albedo.getSampler(); }
    vk::ImageView getNormalView() const { return m_normal.getImageView(); }
    vk::Sampler getNormalSampler() const { return m_normal.getSampler(); }
    vk::ImageView getMetallicRoughnessView() const { return m_metallicRoughness.getImageView(); }
    vk::Sampler getMetallicRoughnessSampler() const { return m_metallicRoughness.getSampler(); }
    vk::ImageView getAoView() const { return m_ao.getImageView(); }
    vk::Sampler getAoSampler() const { return m_ao.getSampler(); }
    vk::ImageView getLightmapView() const { return m_lightmap.getImageView(); }
    vk::Sampler getLightmapSampler() const { return m_lightmap.getSampler(); }
    bool hasLightmap() const { return m_hasLightmap; }

    void createDescriptorSet(vk::Device device, vk::DescriptorPool pool,
        vk::DescriptorSetLayout layout,
        const std::vector<vk::DescriptorBufferInfo>& uboInfos,
        const std::vector<vk::DescriptorBufferInfo>& lightInfos,
        vk::ImageView shadowView, vk::Sampler shadowSampler,
        const std::array<vk::ImageView, 3>& pointShadowViews,
        const std::array<vk::Sampler, 3>& pointShadowSamplers,
        uint32_t frameCount);
    
    vk::DescriptorSet getDescriptorSet(uint32_t frame) const { return m_descriptorSets[frame]; }

    void cleanup();
    
private:
    Texture m_albedo;
    Texture m_normal;
    Texture m_metallicRoughness;
    Texture m_ao;
    Texture m_lightmap;
    bool m_hasLightmap = false;
    std::vector<vk::DescriptorSet> m_descriptorSets;
};

===== Engine/include/Scene/Mesh.h =====
#pragma once
#include "Vulkan/VertexBuffer.h"
#include "Vulkan/IndexBuffer.h"
#include "Scene/Submesh.h"
#include <string>
#include <vector>

class Mesh
{
public:
    void load(vk::Device device, vk::PhysicalDevice physicalDevice, const std::string& path, bool keepCpuData = false);
    void loadFromData(vk::Device device, vk::PhysicalDevice physicalDevice,
        const std::vector<Vertex>& vertices,
        const std::vector<uint32_t>& indices,
        const std::vector<Submesh>& submeshes,
        bool keepCpuData = false);
    
    void cleanup();

    void bind(vk::CommandBuffer cmd) const;
    void drawSubmesh(vk::CommandBuffer cmd, const Submesh& sm) const;

    const std::vector<Submesh>& getSubmeshes() const { return m_submeshes; }

    bool hasCpuData() const { return !m_cpuVertices.empty(); }
    const std::vector<Vertex>& getCpuVertices() const { return m_cpuVertices; }
    const std::vector<uint32_t>& getCpuIndices() const { return m_cpuIndices; }

private:
    VertexBuffer m_vertexBuffer;
    IndexBuffer  m_indexBuffer;
    std::vector<Submesh> m_submeshes;

    std::vector<Vertex> m_cpuVertices;
    std::vector<uint32_t> m_cpuIndices;
};

===== Engine/include/Scene/Scene.h =====
#pragma once
#include "Scene/GameObject.h"
#include <vector>
#include <memory>

class Scene
{
public:
    GameObject& addObject()
    {
        m_pendingObjects.push_back(std::make_shared<GameObject>());
        return *m_pendingObjects.back();
    }

    void removeObject(const std::shared_ptr<GameObject>& obj)
    {
        m_objects.erase(std::remove(m_objects.begin(), m_objects.end(), obj), m_objects.end());
    }

    void flush() { flushPenging(); }

    const std::vector<std::shared_ptr<GameObject>>& getObjects() const { return m_objects; }

    GameObject* findByName(const std::string& name) const
    {
        for (const auto& obj : m_objects)
            if (obj->name == name)
                return obj.get();
        return nullptr;
    }

    void updateComponents(float deltaTime)
    {
        flushPenging();
        for (auto& obj : m_objects)
            obj->updateComponents(deltaTime);
    }

    void fixedUpdateComponents(float fixedDeltaTime)
    {
        flushPenging();
        for (auto& obj : m_objects)
            obj->fixedUpdateComponents(fixedDeltaTime);
        flushPenging();
    }
    
private:
    void flushPenging()
    {
        if (m_pendingObjects.empty()) return;
        for (auto& obj : m_pendingObjects)
            m_objects.push_back(std::move(obj));
        m_pendingObjects.clear();
    }

    std::vector<std::shared_ptr<GameObject>> m_objects;
    std::vector<std::shared_ptr<GameObject>> m_pendingObjects;
};

===== Engine/include/Scene/ScreenSpaceDecal.h =====
#pragma once

        vk::CommandPool pool, vk::Queue queue, 
        const std::string& albedoPath,
        const std::string& opacityPath = "",
        const std::string& normalPath = "",
        const std::string& mrPath = "");

    glm::mat4 getModelMatrix() const;

    void createDescriptorSet(vk::Device device, vk::DescriptorPool pool, vk::DescriptorSetLayout layout,
        vk::ImageView depthView, vk::Sampler depthSampler,
        const DecalCameraBuffer& cameraBuffer, const LightBuffer& lightBuffer, uint32_t frameCount);

===== Engine/include/Scene/Transform.h =====
#pragma once
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <glm/gtc/matrix_transform.hpp>

struct Transform
{
    glm::vec3 position{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 rotation{0.0f};
    glm::vec3 scale{1.0f};

    glm::mat4 getMatrix() const 
    {
        glm::mat4 m = glm::mat4(1.0f);

        m = glm::translate(m, position);

        m = glm::rotate(m, glm::radians(rotation.y), glm::vec3(0, 1, 0));
        m = glm::rotate(m, glm::radians(rotation.x), glm::vec3(1, 0, 0));
        m = glm::rotate(m, glm::radians(rotation.z), glm::vec3(0, 0, 1));

        m = glm::scale(m, scale);

        return m;
    }

    glm::mat4 getNormalMatrix() const
    {
        return glm::transpose(glm::inverse(getMatrix()));
    }
};

#pragma once
#include <vulkan/vulkan.hpp>

enum class AAMethod
{
    None,
    FXAA,
    TAA
};

class AntiAliasing
{

    virtual ~AntiAliasing() = default;

    virtual void initialize(vk::Device device, vk::PhysicalDevice physicalDevice, vk::Extent2D extent, vk::PipelineCache cache) = 0;
    virtual void cleanup() = 0;

    virtual void apply(vk::CommandBuffer cmd, uint32_t frameIndex, vk::ImageView srcView, vk::Sampler srcSampler) = 0;

    virtual AAMethod getMethod() const = 0;
    
};

===== Engine/include/Vulkan/Pipeline.h =====
#pragma once
#include "vulkan/vulkan.hpp"
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

struct PipelineConfig
{
    std::string vertexShader;
    std::string fragmentShader = "";
    vk::RenderPass renderPass;
    vk::PipelineCache cache = nullptr;
    uint32_t frameCount = 1;
    uint32_t maxMaterials = 100;
    uint32_t pushConstantSize = sizeof(glm::mat4) * 2;

    vk::BlendFactor blendSrcFactor = vk::BlendFactor::eOne;
    vk::BlendFactor blendDstFactor = vk::BlendFactor::eZero;
    vk::BlendOp blendOp = vk::BlendOp::eAdd;

    vk::ShaderStageFlags pushStage = vk::ShaderStageFlagBits::eVertex;

    bool useDescriptors = true;
    vk::CullModeFlagBits cullMode = vk::CullModeFlagBits::eBack;
    bool depthClampEnable = false;
    uint32_t colorAttachmentCount = 1;
    bool depthWrite = true;
    bool depthTest = true;
    bool blendEnable = false;

    bool fogLayout = false;

    bool decalLayout = false;

    bool useVertexInput = true;

    bool postProcess = false;

    bool depthBiasEnable = false;
    float depthBiasConstant = 0.0f;
    float depthBiasSlope = 0.0f;
};

class Pipeline
{
public:
    void initialize(vk::Device device, const PipelineConfig& config);
    void cleanup();

    vk::DescriptorSetLayout getDescriptorSetLayout() const { return m_descriptorSetLayout.get(); }
    vk::DescriptorPool getDescriptorPool() const { return m_descriptorPool.get(); }
    vk::PipelineLayout getLayout() const { return m_pipelineLayout.get(); }
    vk::Pipeline getPipeline() const { return m_graphicPipeline.get(); }

private:
    vk::UniquePipelineLayout m_pipelineLayout;
    vk::UniquePipeline m_graphicPipeline;
    vk::UniqueDescriptorSetLayout m_descriptorSetLayout;
    vk::UniqueDescriptorPool m_descriptorPool;
    std::vector<vk::DescriptorSet> m_descriptorSets;
};

    const LightBuffer& getLightBuffer() const { return m_lightBuffer; }
    const DecalCameraBuffer& getDecalCameraBuffer() const { return m_decalCameraBuffer; }

===== Engine/include/Vulkan/Vertex.h =====
#pragma once
#include <vulkan/vulkan.hpp>
#include <glm/glm.hpp>
#include <array>

struct Vertex
{
    glm::vec3 pos;
    glm::vec3 color;
    glm::vec2 texCoord;
    glm::vec3 normal;
    glm::vec3 tangent;
    glm::vec2 lightmapUV;

    bool operator==(const Vertex& other) const
    {
        return pos == other.pos && color == other.color && 
            texCoord == other.texCoord && normal == other.normal;
    }

    static vk::VertexInputBindingDescription getBindingDescriptor()
    {
        vk::VertexInputBindingDescription binding{};
        binding.setBinding(0);
        binding.setStride(sizeof(Vertex));
        binding.setInputRate(vk::VertexInputRate::eVertex);
        return binding;
    }

    static std::array<vk::VertexInputAttributeDescription, 6> getAttributeDescriptions()
    {
        std::array<vk::VertexInputAttributeDescription, 6> attributes{};

        attributes[0].setBinding(0);
        attributes[0].setLocation(0);
        attributes[0].setFormat(vk::Format::eR32G32B32Sfloat);
        attributes[0].setOffset(offsetof(Vertex, pos));

        attributes[1].setBinding(0);
        attributes[1].setLocation(1);
        attributes[1].setFormat(vk::Format::eR32G32B32Sfloat);
        attributes[1].setOffset(offsetof(Vertex, color));

        attributes[2].setBinding(0);
        attributes[2].setLocation(2);
        attributes[2].setFormat(vk::Format::eR32G32Sfloat);
        attributes[2].setOffset(offsetof(Vertex, texCoord));

        attributes[3].setBinding(0);
        attributes[3].setLocation(3);
        attributes[3].setFormat(vk::Format::eR32G32B32Sfloat);
        attributes[3].setOffset(offsetof(Vertex, normal));

        attributes[4].setBinding(0);
        attributes[4].setLocation(4);
        attributes[4].setFormat(vk::Format::eR32G32B32Sfloat);
        attributes[4].setOffset(offsetof(Vertex, tangent));

        attributes[5].setBinding(0);
        attributes[5].setLocation(5);
        attributes[5].setFormat(vk::Format::eR32G32Sfloat);
        attributes[5].setOffset(offsetof(Vertex, lightmapUV));

        return attributes;
    }
};

namespace std
{
    template<> struct hash<Vertex>
    {
        size_t operator()(const Vertex& v) const
        {
            size_t h1 = hash<float>()(v.pos.x) ^ (hash<float>()(v.pos.y) << 1) ^ (hash<float>()(v.pos.z) << 2);
            size_t h2 = hash<float>()(v.texCoord.x) ^ (hash<float>()(v.texCoord.y) << 1);
            size_t h3 = hash<float>()(v.normal.x) ^ (hash<float>()(v.normal.z) << 1);
            return h1 ^ (h2 << 3) ^ (h3 << 6);
        }
    };
}

===== Engine/include/Vulkan/Window.h =====


===== Engine/shaders/common/brdf.glsl =====
#ifndef BRDF_GLSL
#define BRDF_GLSL

const float PI = 3.14159265359;

float DistributionGGX(vec3 N, vec3 H, float rough)
{
    float a = rough * rough;
    float a2 = a * a;
    float NdotH = max(dot(N, H), 0.0);
    float NdotH2 = NdotH * NdotH;
    float denom = (NdotH2 * (a2 - 1.0) + 1.0);
    denom = PI * denom * denom;
    return a2 / max(denom, 1e-6);
}

float GeometrySchlickGGX(float NdotV, float rough)
{
    float r = rough + 1.0;
    float k = (r * r) / 8.0;
    return NdotV / (NdotV * (1.0 - k) + k);
}

float GeometrySmith(vec3 N, vec3 V, vec3 L, float rough)
{
    float NdotV = max(dot(N, V), 0.0);
    float NdotL = max(dot(N, L), 0.0);
    return GeometrySchlickGGX(NdotV, rough) * GeometrySchlickGGX(NdotL, rough);
}

vec3 FresnelSchlick(float cosTheta, vec3 F0)
{
    return F0 + (1.0 - F0) * pow(clamp(1.0 - cosTheta, 0.0, 1.0), 5.0);
}
vec3 FresnelSchlickRoughness(float cosTheta, vec3 F0, float roughness)
{
    return F0 + (max(vec3(1.0 - roughness), F0) - F0) * pow(clamp(1.0 - cosTheta, 0.0, 1.0), 5.0);
}

#endif

===== Engine/shaders/common/tonemap.glsl =====
#ifndef TONEMAP_GLSL
#define TONEMAP_GLSL

vec3 tonemapReinhard(vec3 color)
{
    color = color / (1.0 + color);
    return pow(color, vec3(1.0 / 2.2));
}

vec3 tonemapACES(vec3 color)
{
    const float a = 2.51;
    const float b = 0.03;
    const float c = 2.43;
    const float d = 0.59;
    const float e = 0.14;
    vec3 mapped = clamp((color * (a * color + b)) / (color * (c * color + d) + e), 0.0, 1.0);
    return pow(mapped, vec3(1.0 / 2.2));
}
vec3 tonemapACESSoft(vec3 color, float contrast)
{
    vec3 filmic = tonemapACES(color);
    vec3 linear = clamp(pow(color, vec3(1.0 / 2.2)), 0.0, 1.0);
    return mix(linear, filmic, clamp(contrast, 0.0, 1.0));
}

#endif

===== Engine/shaders/pbr/decal_box.frag =====
#version 450
#extension GL_GOOGLE_include_directive : require
#include "brdf.glsl"

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D decalTexture;      // albedo.rgb + базовая opacity в .a
layout(binding = 1) uniform sampler2D depthTexture;

layout(binding = 2) uniform Camera {
    mat4 view;
    mat4 proj;
    mat4 invViewProj;
    vec4 camPos;
} cam;

struct Light { vec4 position; vec4 color; };
layout(binding = 3) uniform LightUBO {
    Light lights[16];
    ivec4 count;
} lightData;

layout(binding = 4) uniform sampler2D opacityTexture;
layout(binding = 5) uniform sampler2D normalTexture;
layout(binding = 6) uniform sampler2D mrTexture;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push {
    mat4 model;
    mat4 invModel;
    vec4 params;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec4 fragClipPos;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

void main()
{
    vec2 screenUV = (fragClipPos.xy / fragClipPos.w) * 0.5 + 0.5;
    float sceneDepth = texture(depthTexture, screenUV).r;

    vec4 ndc = vec4(screenUV * 2.0 - 1.0, sceneDepth, 1.0);
    vec4 worldPosH = cam.invViewProj * ndc;
    vec3 worldPos = worldPosH.xyz / worldPosH.w;

    vec3 localPos = (push.invModel * vec4(worldPos, 1.0)).xyz;
    if (any(greaterThan(abs(localPos), vec3(0.5))))
        discard;

    vec2 decalUV = localPos.xz + 0.5;

    vec4 albedoTex = texture(decalTexture, decalUV);
    float opacity = albedoTex.a * texture(opacityTexture, decalUV).r;
    if (opacity < 0.01)
        discard;

    vec3 albedo = pow(albedoTex.rgb, vec3(2.2));

    mat3 normalMat = mat3(transpose(inverse(push.model)));
    vec3 T  = normalize(normalMat * vec3(1.0, 0.0, 0.0));
    vec3 B  = normalize(normalMat * vec3(0.0, 0.0, 1.0));
    vec3 N0 = normalize(normalMat * vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0));
    mat3 TBN = mat3(T, B, N0);

    vec3 nSample = texture(normalTexture, decalUV).rgb * 2.0 - 1.0;
    vec3 N = normalize(TBN * nSample);

    vec3 mr = texture(mrTexture, decalUV).rgb;
    float roughness = clamp(mr.g * push.params.y, 0.05, 1.0);
    float metallic  = clamp(mr.b * push.params.x, 0.0, 1.0);

    vec3 V = normalize(cam.camPos.xyz - worldPos);
    vec3 F0 = mix(vec3(0.04), albedo, metallic);
    vec3 Lo = vec3(0.0);

    for (int i = 0; i < lightData.count.x; i++)
    {
        Light light = lightData.lights[i];
        vec3 L, radiance;

        if (light.position.w < 0.5) {
            L = normalize(-light.position.xyz);
            radiance = light.color.rgb * light.color.a;
        } else {
            vec3 toLight = light.position.xyz - worldPos;
            float dist2 = max(dot(toLight, toLight), 0.01);
            L = normalize(toLight);
            radiance = light.color.rgb * light.color.a / dist2;
        }

        vec3 H = normalize(V + L);
        float D = DistributionGGX(N, H, roughness);
        float G = GeometrySmith(N, V, L, roughness);
        vec3  F = FresnelSchlick(max(dot(H, V), 0.0), F0);

        vec3 specular = (D * G * F) / (4.0 * max(dot(N, V), 0.0) * max(dot(N, L), 0.0) + 0.0001);
        vec3 kD = (1.0 - F) * (1.0 - metallic);
        float NdotL = max(dot(N, L), 0.0);

        Lo += (kD * albedo / PI + specular) * radiance * NdotL;
    }

    vec3 ambient = vec3(0.02) * albedo;
    outColor = vec4(ambient + Lo, opacity);
}

===== Engine/shaders/pbr/decal_box.vert =====
#version 450

layout(binding = 2) uniform Camera {
    mat4 view;
    mat4 proj;
    mat4 invViewProj;
} cam;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push {
    mat4 model;
    mat4 invModel;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec3 inPosition;

layout(location = 0) out vec4 fragClipPos;

void main()
{
    vec4 worldPos = push.model * vec4(inPosition, 1.0);
    gl_Position = cam.proj * cam.view * worldPos;
    fragClipPos = gl_Position;
}

===== Engine/shaders/pbr/pbr.frag =====
#version 450
#extension GL_GOOGLE_include_directive : require

#include "brdf.glsl"
#include "tonemap.glsl"

layout(binding = 0) uniform Camera {
    mat4 view;
    mat4 proj;
    mat4 lightSpaceMatrix;
    mat4 prevViewProj;
    mat4 viewProjNoJitter;
    vec4 camPos;
} cam;
layout(binding = 1) uniform sampler2D albedoMap;
layout(binding = 2) uniform sampler2D normalMap;
layout(binding = 3) uniform sampler2D metallicRoughnessMap;
layout(binding = 4) uniform sampler2D aoMap;

struct Light {
    vec4 position;
    vec4 color;
};

layout(binding = 5) uniform LightUBO {
    Light lights[16];
    ivec4 count;
} lightData;

layout(binding = 6) uniform sampler2DShadow shadowMap;
layout(binding = 7) uniform samplerCube pointShadowMap0;
layout(binding = 9) uniform samplerCube pointShadowMap1;
layout(binding = 10) uniform samplerCube pointShadowMap2;
layout(binding = 8) uniform sampler2D lightmapMap;

layout(location = 0) in vec3 fragWorldPos;
layout(location = 1) in vec2 fragTexCoord;
layout(location = 2) in vec4 fragLightSpacePos;
layout(location = 3) in mat3 fragTBN;

layout(location = 6) in vec4 fragCurrClipPos;
layout(location = 7) in vec4 fragPrevClipPos;
layout(location = 8) in vec2 fragLightmapUV;

layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;
layout(location = 1) out vec2 outVelocity;

const vec3 SKY_ZENITH  = vec3(0.20, 0.32, 0.55);
const vec3 SKY_HORIZON = vec3(0.55, 0.60, 0.65);
const vec3 GROUND      = vec3(0.05, 0.05, 0.06);

const float POINT_SHADOW_FAR_PLANE = 25.0;

vec3 sampleSky(vec3 dir)
{
    float t = smoothstep(0.0, 1.0, dir.y * 0.5 + 0.5);
    vec3 upperSky = mix(SKY_HORIZON, SKY_ZENITH, t);
    return mix(GROUND, upperSky, smoothstep(-0.05, 0.05, dir.y));
}

float interleavedGradientNoise(vec2 p)
{
    const vec3 magic = vec3(0.06711056, 0.00583715, 52.9829189);
    return fract(magic.z * fract(dot(p, magic.xy)));
}

float calculateShadow(vec4 lightSpacePos, vec3 N, vec3 L, vec2 screenXY)
{
    vec3 proj = lightSpacePos.xyz / lightSpacePos.w;
    proj.xy = proj.xy * 0.5 + 0.5;

    if (proj.x < 0.0 || proj.x > 1.0 ||
        proj.y < 0.0 || proj.y > 1.0 ||
        proj.z < 0.0 || proj.z > 1.0)
    {
        return 1.0;
    }

    float bias = max(0.0025 * (1.0 - dot(N, L)), 0.0006);
    vec2 texelSize = 1.0 / vec2(textureSize(shadowMap, 0));

    float angle = interleavedGradientNoise(screenXY) * 6.2831853;
    float s = sin(angle), c = cos(angle);
    mat2 rot = mat2(c, -s, s, c);

    float shadow = 0.0;
    const int R = 1;
    for (int x = -R; x <= R; x++)
    {
        for (int y = -R; y <= R; y++)
        {
            vec2 offset = rot * (vec2(x, y) * texelSize);
            shadow += texture(shadowMap, vec3(proj.xy + offset, proj.z - bias));
        }
    }
    float samples = float((2 * R + 1) * (2 * R + 1));
    return shadow / samples;
}

float calculatePointShadow(vec3 fragPos, vec3 lightPos, vec3 N, vec2 screenXY, int shadowSlot)
{
    vec3 toFrag = fragPos - lightPos;
    float currentDist = length(toFrag);
    vec3 L = normalize(-toFrag);

    float bias = max(0.15 * (1.0 - dot(N, L)), 0.03);

    const int SAMPLES = 20;
    vec3 sampleOffsets[SAMPLES] = vec3[]
    (
        vec3( 1,  1,  1), vec3( 1, -1,  1), vec3(-1, -1,  1), vec3(-1,  1,  1),
        vec3( 1,  1, -1), vec3( 1, -1, -1), vec3(-1, -1, -1), vec3(-1,  1, -1),
        vec3( 1,  1,  0), vec3( 1, -1,  0), vec3(-1, -1,  0), vec3(-1,  1,  0),
        vec3( 1,  0,  1), vec3(-1,  0,  1), vec3( 1,  0, -1), vec3(-1,  0, -1),
        vec3( 0,  1,  1), vec3( 0, -1,  1), vec3( 0, -1, -1), vec3( 0,  1, -1)
    );

    float angle = interleavedGradientNoise(screenXY) * 6.2831853;
    float s = sin(angle), c = cos(angle);
    vec3 axis = L;

    mat3 rot = mat3(
        c + axis.x*axis.x*(1.0-c),        axis.x*axis.y*(1.0-c) - axis.z*s, axis.x*axis.z*(1.0-c) + axis.y*s,
        axis.y*axis.x*(1.0-c) + axis.z*s, c + axis.y*axis.y*(1.0-c),        axis.y*axis.z*(1.0-c) - axis.x*s,
        axis.z*axis.x*(1.0-c) - axis.y*s, axis.z*axis.y*(1.0-c) + axis.x*s, c + axis.z*axis.z*(1.0-c)
    );

    float shadow = 0.0;
    float diskRadius = (1.0 + (currentDist / POINT_SHADOW_FAR_PLANE)) / 15.0;

    for (int i = 0; i < SAMPLES; i++)
    {
        vec3 offset = rot * sampleOffsets[i];
        float closestDist;

        if (shadowSlot == 0)
            closestDist = texture(pointShadowMap0, toFrag + offset * diskRadius).r * POINT_SHADOW_FAR_PLANE;
        else if (shadowSlot == 1)
            closestDist = texture(pointShadowMap1, toFrag + offset * diskRadius).r * POINT_SHADOW_FAR_PLANE;
        else
            closestDist = texture(pointShadowMap2, toFrag + offset * diskRadius).r * POINT_SHADOW_FAR_PLANE;

        if (currentDist - bias > closestDist)
            shadow += 1.0;
    }

    return shadow / float(SAMPLES);
}

void main()
{
    vec3 albedo = pow(texture(albedoMap, fragTexCoord).rgb, vec3(2.2));
    vec3 mr = texture(metallicRoughnessMap, fragTexCoord).rgb;
    float roughness = clamp(mr.g, 0.4, 1.0);
    float metallic = mr.b;
    float ao = texture(aoMap, fragTexCoord).r;

    vec3 N = texture(normalMap, fragTexCoord).rgb;
    N = N * 2.0 - 1.0;
    N = normalize(fragTBN * N);

    vec3 V = normalize(cam.camPos.xyz - fragWorldPos);
    vec3 R = reflect(-V, N);
    vec3 F0 = mix(vec3(0.04), albedo, metallic);

    vec3 Lo = vec3(0.0);
    bool pointShadowUsed = false;

    for (int i = 0; i < lightData.count.x; i++)
    {
        Light light = lightData.lights[i];
        vec3 L, radiance;

        if (light.position.w < 0.5) {
            L = normalize(-light.position.xyz);
            radiance = light.color.rgb * light.color.a;
        }
        else
        {
            vec3 toLight = light.position.xyz - fragWorldPos;
            float dist2 = max(dot(toLight, toLight), 0.01);
            L = normalize(toLight);
            float atten = 1.0 / dist2;
            radiance = light.color.rgb * light.color.a * atten;

            int shadowSlot = -1;
            if (i == lightData.count.y) shadowSlot = 0;
            else if (i == lightData.count.z) shadowSlot = 1;
            else if (i == lightData.count.w) shadowSlot = 2;
            
            if (shadowSlot >= 0)
            {
                float pShadow = calculatePointShadow(fragWorldPos, light.position.xyz, N, gl_FragCoord.xy, shadowSlot);
                radiance *= (1.0 - pShadow);
            }
        }

        float NdotL = max(dot(N, L), 0.0);
        float NdotV = max(dot(N, V), 0.0);

        if (NdotL > 0.0)
        {
            vec3 H = normalize(V + L);
            float D = DistributionGGX(N, H, roughness);
            float G = GeometrySmith(N, V, L, roughness);
            vec3 F = FresnelSchlick(max(dot(H, V), 0.0), F0);

            vec3 numerator = D * G * F;
            float denominator = 4.0 * NdotV * NdotL + 0.0001;
            vec3 specular = numerator / denominator;
            specular = min(specular, vec3(8.0));

            vec3 kD = (1.0 - F) * (1.0 - metallic);

            float visibility = 1.0;
            if (i == 0 && light.position.w < 0.5) {
                vec3 geoN = normalize(fragTBN[2]);
                visibility = calculateShadow(fragLightSpacePos, geoN, L, gl_FragCoord.xy);
            }

            Lo += (kD * albedo / PI + specular) * radiance * NdotL * visibility;
        }
    }

    vec3 ambient = vec3(0.005) * albedo * ao;
    vec3 color = ambient + Lo;

    vec3 lightmapColor = texture(lightmapMap, fragLightmapUV).rgb;
    color += lightmapColor * 0.3;

    float alpha = texture(albedoMap, fragTexCoord).a;
    outColor = vec4(color, alpha);

    vec2 currNDC = fragCurrClipPos.xy / fragCurrClipPos.w;
    vec2 prevNDC = fragPrevClipPos.xy / fragPrevClipPos.w;
    outVelocity = (currNDC - prevNDC) * 0.5;
}

===== Engine/shaders/pbr/pbr.vert =====
#version 450
#extension GL_GOOGLE_include_directive : require

layout(binding = 0) uniform Camera {
    mat4 view;
    mat4 proj;
    mat4 lightSpaceMatrix;
    mat4 prevViewProj;
    mat4 viewProjNoJitter;
    vec4 camPos;
} cam;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push
{
    mat4 model;
    mat4 normalMatrix;
    mat4 prevModel;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec3 inPosition;
layout(location = 1) in vec3 inColor;
layout(location = 2) in vec2 inTexCoord;
layout(location = 3) in vec3 inNormal;
layout(location = 4) in vec3 inTangent;
layout(location = 5) in vec2 inLightmapUV;

layout(location = 0) out vec3 fragWorldPos;
layout(location = 1) out vec2 fragTexCoord;
layout(location = 2) out vec4 fragLightSpacePos;
layout(location = 3) out mat3 fragTBN;
layout(location = 6) out vec4 fragCurrClipPos;
layout(location = 7) out vec4 fragPrevClipPos;
layout(location = 8) out vec2 fragLightmapUV;

void main()
{
    vec4 worldPos = push.model * vec4(inPosition, 1.0);
    gl_Position = cam.proj * cam.view * worldPos;

    fragWorldPos = worldPos.xyz;
    fragTexCoord = inTexCoord;
    fragLightSpacePos = cam.lightSpaceMatrix * worldPos;

    fragCurrClipPos = cam.viewProjNoJitter * worldPos;

    vec4 prevWorldPos = push.prevModel * vec4(inPosition, 1.0);
    fragPrevClipPos = cam.prevViewProj * prevWorldPos;

    fragLightmapUV = inLightmapUV;

    mat3 nm = mat3(push.normalMatrix);
    vec3 N = normalize(nm * inNormal);
    vec3 T = normalize(nm * inTangent);
    T = normalize(T - dot(T, N) * N);
    vec3 B = cross(N, T);
    fragTBN = mat3(T, B, N);
}

===== Engine/shaders/post/blur.frag =====
#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D image;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push 
{
    vec2 direction;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

void main()
{
    vec2 texelSize = 1.0 / vec2(textureSize(image, 0));
    vec3 result = vec3(0.0);

    float weights[5] = float[](0.227027, 0.1945946, 0.1216216, 0.054054, 0.016216);

    result += texture(image, fragUV).rgb * weights[0];

    for (int i = 1; i < 5; i++)
    {
        vec2 offset = push.direction * texelSize * float(i);
        result += texture(image, fragUV + offset).rgb * weights[i];
        result += texture(image, fragUV - offset).rgb * weights[i];
    }

    outColor = vec4(result, 1.0);    
}

===== Engine/shaders/post/brightpass.frag =====
#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D hdrBuffer;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push
{
    float threshold;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

void main()
{
    vec3 color = texture(hdrBuffer, fragUV).rgb;
    float brightness = dot(color, vec3(0.2126, 0.7152, 0.0722));

    float knee = push.threshold * 0.5 + 1e-4;
    float soft = clamp(brightness - push.threshold + knee, 0.0, 2.0 * knee);
    soft = soft * soft / (4.0 * knee);

    float contribution = max(soft, brightness - push.threshold);
    contribution /= max(brightness, 1e-4);

    outColor = vec4(color * contribution, 1.0);
}

===== Engine/shaders/post/downsample.frag =====
#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D srcTexture;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push 
{
    vec2 scrTexelSize;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

void main()
{
    vec2 uv = fragUV;
    vec2 texel = push.scrTexelSize;

    vec3 a = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2(-1.0, -1.0)).rgb;
    vec3 b = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2( 0.0, -1.0)).rgb;
    vec3 c = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2( 1.0, -1.0)).rgb;
    vec3 d = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2(-0.5, -0.5)).rgb;
    vec3 e = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2( 0.5, -0.5)).rgb;
    vec3 f = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2(-1.0,  0.0)).rgb;
    vec3 g = texture(srcTexture, uv).rgb;
    vec3 h = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2( 1.0,  0.0)).rgb;
    vec3 i = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2(-0.5,  0.5)).rgb;
    vec3 j = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2( 0.5,  0.5)).rgb;
    vec3 k = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2(-1.0,  1.0)).rgb;
    vec3 l = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2( 0.0,  1.0)).rgb;
    vec3 m = texture(srcTexture, uv + texel * vec2( 1.0,  1.0)).rgb;

    vec3 result = (d + e + i + j) * 0.125;
    result += (a + b + f + g) * 0.03125;
    result += (b + c + g + h) * 0.03125;
    result += (f + g + k + l) * 0.03125;
    result += (g + h + l + m) * 0.03125;

    outColor = vec4(result, 1.0);    
}

===== Engine/shaders/post/fog.frag =====
#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D depthTexture;

layout(binding = 1) uniform Camera {
    mat4 view;
    mat4 proj;
    mat4 invViewProj;
    mat4 lightSpaceMatrix;
    vec4 camPos;
} cam;

layout(binding = 2) uniform sampler2DShadow shadowMap;

struct Light { vec4 position; vec4 color; };
layout(binding = 3) uniform LightUBO {
    Light lights[16];
    ivec4 count;
} lightData;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push {
    vec4 scatteringColorDensity;
    vec4 heightParams;
    vec4 lightParams;
    vec4 miscParams;
} fog;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

float hash(vec3 p)
{
    p = fract(p * 0.3183099 + vec3(0.1, 0.2, 0.3));
    p *= 17.0;
    return fract(p.x * p.y * p.z * (p.x + p.y + p.z));
}

float sampleSunShadow(vec3 worldPos)
{
    vec4 lsPos = cam.lightSpaceMatrix * vec4(worldPos, 1.0);
    vec3 proj = lsPos.xyz / lsPos.w;
    proj.xy = proj.xy * 0.5 + 0.5;

    if (proj.x < 0.0 || proj.x > 1.0 || proj.y < 0.0 || proj.y > 1.0 || proj.z < 0.0 || proj.z > 1.0)
        return 1.0;

    return texture(shadowMap, vec3(proj.xy, proj.z - 0.001));
}

float henyeyGreenstein(float cosAngle, float g)
{
    float g2 = g * g;
    return (1.0 - g2) / (4.0 * 3.14159265 * pow(1.0 + g2 - 2.0 * g * cosAngle, 1.5));
}

void main()
{
    vec3 scatteringColor = fog.scatteringColorDensity.rgb;
    float density        = fog.scatteringColorDensity.a;
    float heightFalloff   = fog.heightParams.x;
    float heightStart     = fog.heightParams.y;
    float maxDistance     = fog.heightParams.z;
    int   stepCount       = int(fog.heightParams.w);
    float ambientIntensity = fog.lightParams.x;
    float sunIntensity     = fog.lightParams.y;
    float noiseScale       = fog.lightParams.z;
    float noiseSpeed       = fog.lightParams.w;
    float time              = fog.miscParams.x;

    float sceneDepth = texture(depthTexture, fragUV).r;

    if (sceneDepth >= 0.9999)
    {
        outColor = vec4(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
        return;
    }
    
    vec4 ndc = vec4(fragUV * 2.0 - 1.0, sceneDepth, 1.0);
    vec4 worldPosH = cam.invViewProj * ndc;
    vec3 scenePos = worldPosH.xyz / worldPosH.w;

    vec3 camPos = cam.camPos.xyz;
    vec3 toScene = scenePos - camPos;
    float sceneDist = length(toScene);
    vec3 rayDir = toScene / max(sceneDist, 0.0001);

    float rayLength = min(sceneDist, maxDistance);
    float dt = rayLength / float(stepCount);

    vec3 sunColor = vec3(0.0);
    float sunPower = 0.0;
    if (lightData.count.x > 0 && lightData.lights[0].position.w < 0.5)
    {
        sunColor = lightData.lights[0].color.rgb;
        sunPower = lightData.lights[0].color.a;
    }

    vec3 pos = camPos;
    float transmittance = 1.0;
    vec3 inscatter = vec3(0.0);

    for (int i = 0; i < stepCount; i++)
    {
        pos += rayDir * dt;

        float heightAtten = exp(-max(pos.y - heightStart, 0.0) * heightFalloff);
        float n = hash(pos * noiseScale + vec3(time * noiseSpeed, 0.0, 0.0));
        float localDensity = density * heightAtten * mix(0.6, 1.4, n);

        float stepTransmittance = exp(-localDensity * dt);

        float visibility = sampleSunShadow(pos);
        vec3 sunDir = vec3(0.0, 1.0, 0.0);
        if (lightData.count.x > 0 && lightData.lights[0].position.w < 0.5)
            sunDir = normalize(-lightData.lights[0].position.xyz);

        float cosAngle = dot(rayDir, sunDir);
        float phase = henyeyGreenstein(cosAngle, 0.6);
        vec3 sunContribution = sunColor * sunPower * visibility * sunIntensity * phase;
        vec3 ambientContribution = scatteringColor * ambientIntensity;

        vec3 stepInscatter = (sunContribution + ambientContribution) * scatteringColor * localDensity * dt;

        inscatter += stepInscatter * transmittance;
        transmittance *= stepTransmittance;
    }

    float dither = (hash(vec3(gl_FragCoord.xy, 0.0)) - 0.5) / 255.0;
    outColor = vec4(inscatter + dither, 1.0 - transmittance);
}

===== Engine/shaders/post/fog.vert =====
#version 450

layout(location = 0) out vec2 fragUV;

void main()
{
    fragUV = vec2((gl_VertexIndex << 1) & 2, gl_VertexIndex & 2);
    gl_Position = vec4(fragUV * 2.0 - 1.0, 0.0, 1.0);
}

===== Engine/shaders/post/fullscreen.vert =====
#version 450

layout(location = 0) out vec2 fragUV;

void main()
{
    fragUV = vec2((gl_VertexIndex << 1) & 2, gl_VertexIndex & 2);
    gl_Position = vec4(fragUV * 2.0 - 1.0, 0.0, 1.0);
}

===== Engine/shaders/post/fxaa.frag =====
#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D srcTexture;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

// FXAA Quality Settings
#define FXAA_SPAN_MAX 8.0
#define FXAA_REDUCE_MUL 1.0/8.0
#define FXAA_REDUCE_MIN 1.0/128.0
#define FXAA_SUBPIX_QUALITY 0.75

float luma(vec3 color)
{
    return dot(color, vec3(0.299, 0.587, 0.114));
}

void main()
{
    vec2 texelSize = 1.0 / vec2(textureSize(srcTexture, 0));
    
    vec3 rgbNW = texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texelSize * vec2(-1.0, -1.0)).rgb;
    vec3 rgbNE = texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texelSize * vec2( 1.0, -1.0)).rgb;
    vec3 rgbSW = texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texelSize * vec2(-1.0,  1.0)).rgb;
    vec3 rgbSE = texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texelSize * vec2( 1.0,  1.0)).rgb;
    vec3 rgbM  = texture(srcTexture, fragUV).rgb;
    
    float lumaNW = luma(rgbNW);
    float lumaNE = luma(rgbNE);
    float lumaSW = luma(rgbSW);
    float lumaSE = luma(rgbSE);
    float lumaM  = luma(rgbM);
    
    float lumaMin = min(lumaM, min(min(lumaNW, lumaNE), min(lumaSW, lumaSE)));
    float lumaMax = max(lumaM, max(max(lumaNW, lumaNE), max(lumaSW, lumaSE)));
    
    vec2 dir;
    dir.x = -((lumaNW + lumaNE) - (lumaSW + lumaSE));
    dir.y =  ((lumaNW + lumaSW) - (lumaNE + lumaSE));
    
    float dirReduce = max((lumaNW + lumaNE + lumaSW + lumaSE) * (0.25 * FXAA_REDUCE_MUL), FXAA_REDUCE_MIN);
    float rcpDirMin = 1.0 / (min(abs(dir.x), abs(dir.y)) + dirReduce);
    dir = min(vec2(FXAA_SPAN_MAX), max(vec2(-FXAA_SPAN_MAX), dir * rcpDirMin)) * texelSize;
    
    vec3 rgbA = 0.5 * (
        texture(srcTexture, fragUV + dir * (1.0/3.0 - 0.5)).rgb +
        texture(srcTexture, fragUV + dir * (2.0/3.0 - 0.5)).rgb
    );
    vec3 rgbB = rgbA * 0.5 + 0.25 * (
        texture(srcTexture, fragUV + dir * -0.5).rgb +
        texture(srcTexture, fragUV + dir *  0.5).rgb
    );
    
    float lumaB = luma(rgbB);
    
    if (lumaB < lumaMin || lumaB > lumaMax) {
        outColor = vec4(rgbA, 1.0);
    } else {
        outColor = vec4(rgbB, 1.0);
    }
    
    float lumaAverage = (1.0/12.0) * (2.0 * (lumaNW + lumaNE + lumaSW + lumaSE) + lumaM);
    float subpix1 = lumaAverage - lumaM;
    float subpix2 = subpix1 * subpix1;
    float subpix3 = subpix2 * subpix2;
    float subpix = clamp((-subpix3) * 8.0 + FXAA_SUBPIX_QUALITY, 0.0, 1.0);
    
    vec3 finalColor = mix(outColor.rgb, rgbM, subpix);
    outColor = vec4(finalColor, 1.0);
}

===== Engine/shaders/post/godray_blur.frag =====
#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D maskTexture;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push
{
    vec4 sunUVParams;
    vec4 weightCountParams;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

void main()
{
    vec2 sunUV = push.sunUVParams.xy;
    float density = push.sunUVParams.z;
    float decay = push.sunUVParams.w;
    float weight = push.weightCountParams.x;
    int sampleCount = int(push.weightCountParams.y);

    vec2 deltaUV = (fragUV - sunUV) * density / float(sampleCount);

    vec2 uv = fragUV;
    float illuminationDecay = 1.0;
    vec3 result = vec3(0.0);

    for (int i = 0; i < sampleCount; i++)
    {
        uv -= deltaUV;
        vec3 sampleColor = texture(maskTexture, uv).rgb;
        sampleColor *= illuminationDecay * weight;
        result += sampleColor;
        illuminationDecay *= decay;
    }

    outColor = vec4(result, 1.0);
}

===== Engine/shaders/post/godray_composite.frag =====
#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D godrayTexture;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push
{
    float intensity;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

void main()
{
    outColor = vec4(texture(godrayTexture, fragUV).rgb * push.intensity, 1.0);
}

===== Engine/shaders/post/godray_mask.frag =====
#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D depthTexture;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push
{
    mat4 invViewProj;
    vec4 sunDirIntensity;
    vec4 sunColorSize;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

void main()
{
    if (push.sunDirIntensity.w < 0.5)
    {
        outColor = vec4(0.0);
        return;
    }

    float sceneDepth = texture(depthTexture, fragUV).r;

    if (sceneDepth < 0.9999)
    {
        outColor = vec4(0.0);
        return;
    }

    vec2 ndcXY = fragUV * 2.0 - 1.0;
    vec4 nearH = push.invViewProj * vec4(ndcXY, 0.0, 1.0);
    vec4 farH  = push.invViewProj * vec4(ndcXY, 1.0, 1.0);
    vec3 dir = normalize(farH.xyz / farH.w - nearH.xyz / nearH.w);

    float cosAngle = dot(dir, push.sunDirIntensity.xyz);
    float sunSize = push.sunColorSize.a;
    float sunDisk = smoothstep(sunSize, sunSize + 0.0005, cosAngle);

    outColor = vec4(push.sunColorSize.rgb * sunDisk, 1.0);
}

===== Engine/shaders/post/taa.frag =====
#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D currentColor;
layout(binding = 1) uniform sampler2D velocityBuffer;
layout(binding = 2) uniform sampler2D historyColor;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push {
    float blendFactor;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

vec3 rgbToYCoCg(vec3 c)
{
    float y  = dot(c, vec3(0.25, 0.5, 0.25));
    float co = dot(c, vec3(0.5, 0.0, -0.5));
    float cg = dot(c, vec3(-0.25, 0.5, -0.25));
    return vec3(y, co, cg);
}

vec3 yCoCgToRgb(vec3 c)
{
    float y = c.x, co = c.y, cg = c.z;
    return vec3(y + co - cg, y + cg, y - co - cg);
}

void main()
{
    vec3 current = texture(currentColor, fragUV).rgb;

    vec2 velocity = texture(velocityBuffer, fragUV).rg;
    vec2 prevUV = fragUV - velocity;

    if (prevUV.x < 0.0 || prevUV.x > 1.0 || prevUV.y < 0.0 || prevUV.y > 1.0)
    {
        outColor = vec4(current, 1.0);
        return;
    }

    vec3 history = texture(historyColor, prevUV).rgb;

    vec2 texelSize = 1.0 / vec2(textureSize(currentColor, 0));

    vec3 neighborhoodMin = vec3(1e6);
    vec3 neighborhoodMax = vec3(-1e6);

    for (int y = -1; y <= 1; y++)
    {
        for (int x = -1; x <= 1; x++)
        {
            vec3 sampleColor = texture(currentColor, fragUV + vec2(x, y) * texelSize).rgb;
            vec3 sampleYCoCg = rgbToYCoCg(sampleColor);
            neighborhoodMin = min(neighborhoodMin, sampleYCoCg);
            neighborhoodMax = max(neighborhoodMax, sampleYCoCg);
        }
    }

    vec3 historyYCoCg = rgbToYCoCg(history);
    vec3 clampedYCoCg = clamp(historyYCoCg, neighborhoodMin, neighborhoodMax);
    vec3 clampedHistory = yCoCgToRgb(clampedYCoCg);

    vec3 result = mix(current, clampedHistory, push.blendFactor);
    outColor = vec4(result, 1.0);
}

===== Engine/shaders/post/tonemap.frag =====
#version 450
#extension GL_GOOGLE_include_directive : require

#include "tonemap.glsl"

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D hdrBuffer;
layout(binding = 1) uniform sampler2D bloomBuffer;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push {
    float exposure;
    float contrast;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

void main()
{
    vec3 hdrColor = texture(hdrBuffer, fragUV).rgb;
    vec3 bloomColor = texture(bloomBuffer, fragUV).rgb;

    vec3 combined = hdrColor + bloomColor * 0.65;
    combined *= push.exposure;

    vec3 mapped = tonemapACESSoft(combined, push.contrast);
    outColor = vec4(mapped, 1.0);
}

===== Engine/shaders/post/upsample.frag =====
#version 450

layout(binding = 0) uniform sampler2D srcTexture;

layout(push_constant) uniform Push 
{
    float radius;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec2 fragUV;
layout(location = 0) out vec4 outColor;

void main()
{
    vec2 texel = push.radius / vec2(textureSize(srcTexture, 0));

    vec3 result = texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texel * vec2(-1.0, -1.0)).rgb * 1.0;
    result += texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texel * vec2( 0.0, -1.0)).rgb * 2.0;
    result += texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texel * vec2( 1.0, -1.0)).rgb * 1.0;
    result += texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texel * vec2(-1.0,  0.0)).rgb * 2.0;
    result += texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texel * vec2( 0.0,  0.0)).rgb * 4.0;
    result += texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texel * vec2( 1.0,  0.0)).rgb * 2.0;
    result += texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texel * vec2(-1.0,  1.0)).rgb * 1.0;
    result += texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texel * vec2( 0.0,  1.0)).rgb * 2.0;
    result += texture(srcTexture, fragUV + texel * vec2( 1.0,  1.0)).rgb * 1.0;

    outColor = vec4(result / 16.0, 1.0);
}

===== Engine/shaders/shadow/point_shadow.frag =====
#version 450

layout(push_constant) uniform Push
{
    mat4 lightViewProj;
    mat4 model;
    vec4 lightPos;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec3 fragWorldPos;

void main()
{
    float dist = length(fragWorldPos - push.lightPos.xyz);
    gl_FragDepth = dist / push.lightPos.w;
}

===== Engine/shaders/shadow/point_shadow.vert =====
#version 450

layout(push_constant) uniform Push
{
    mat4 lightViewProj;
    mat4 model;
    vec4 lightPos;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec3 inPosition;

layout(location = 0) out vec3 fragWorldPos;

void main()
{
    vec4 worldPos = push.model * vec4(inPosition, 1.0);
    fragWorldPos = worldPos.xyz;
    gl_Position = push.lightViewProj * worldPos;
}

===== Engine/shaders/shadow/shadow.vert =====
#version 450

layout(push_constant) uniform Push {
    mat4 lightSpaceMatrix;
    mat4 model;
} push;

layout(location = 0) in vec3 inPosition;
out gl_PerVertex { vec4 gl_Position; };

void main() {
    vec4 pos = push.lightSpaceMatrix * push.model * vec4(inPosition, 1.0);
    gl_Position = pos;
}

===== Engine/src/Core/Camera.cpp =====
#include "Core/Camera.h"
#define GLM_ENABLE_EXPERIMENTAL
#include <glm/gtx/rotate_vector.hpp>
#include <glm/gtc/matrix_transform.hpp>
#include <algorithm>

void Camera::initialize(glm::vec3 position)
{
    m_position = position;
    updateVectors();
}

void Camera::processKeyboard(glm::vec3 direction, float deltaTime)
{
    float velocity = m_moveSpeed * deltaTime;
    m_position += direction * velocity;
}

void Camera::lookAt(glm::vec3 target)
{
    m_front = glm::normalize(target - m_position);
    m_yaw = glm::degrees(atan2(m_front.z ,m_front.x));
    m_pitch = glm::degrees(asin(m_front.y));
    updateVectors();
}

void Camera::processMouse(float deltaX, float deltaY)
{
    m_yaw += deltaX * m_mouseSensitivity;
    m_pitch += deltaY * m_mouseSensitivity;

    m_pitch = std::clamp(m_pitch, -89.0f, 89.0f);

    updateVectors();
}

glm::mat4 Camera::getViewMatrix() const
{
    glm::vec3 rolledUp = glm::rotate(m_up, glm::radians(m_roll), m_front);
    return glm::lookAt(m_position, m_position + m_front, rolledUp);
}

void Camera::updateVectors()
{
    glm::vec3 front;
    front.x = cos(glm::radians(m_yaw)) * cos(glm::radians(m_pitch));
    front.y = sin(glm::radians(m_pitch));
    front.z = sin(glm::radians(m_yaw)) * cos(glm::radians(m_pitch));

    m_front = glm::normalize(front);
    m_right = glm::normalize(glm::cross(m_front, m_worldUp));
    m_up    = glm::normalize(glm::cross(m_right, m_front));    
}

glm::mat4 Camera::getProjectionMatrix() const
{
    glm::mat4 proj = glm::perspective(
        glm::radians(m_fov),
        m_aspect,
        m_nearPlane,
        m_farPlane
    );
    proj[1][1] *= -1; 
    return proj;
}

===== Engine/src/Core/Engine.cpp =====
#include "Core/Engine.h"
#include "Core/Log.h"
#include "Components/RigidBodyComponent.h"
#include "Components/TransformComponent.h"

std::vector<unsigned char> applyMRFactors(const unsigned char* src, int w, int h,
    float metallicFactor, float roughnessFactor)
{
